SELECT s."Id", s."Duration", s."Equipment", s."ImageUrl", s."Level", s."Name", s."TrainerId", s."TrainingType", s1."ClassesId", s1."TrainersId", s1."Id", s1."Discriminator", s1."Email", s1."Name", s1."PasswordHash", s1."Bio", s1."Specialization"
FROM "SportClasses" AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0."ClassesId", s0."TrainersId", u0."Id", u0."Discriminator", u0."Email", u0."Name", u0."PasswordHash", u0."Bio", u0."Specialization"
    FROM "SportClassTrainer" AS s0
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT u."Id", u."Discriminator", u."Email", u."Name", u."PasswordHash", u."Bio", u."Specialization"
        FROM "Users" AS u
        WHERE u."Discriminator" = 'Trainer'
    ) AS u0 ON s0."TrainersId" = u0."Id"
) AS s1 ON s."Id" = s1."ClassesId"
ORDER BY s."Id", s1."ClassesId", s1."TrainersId"

2026-09-23 00:58:05.459 +03:00 [ERR] Failed executing DbCommand (70ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT s."Id", s."Duration", s."Equipment", s."ImageUrl", s."Level", s."Name", s."TrainerId", s."TrainingType", s1."ClassesId", s1."TrainersId", s1."Id", s1."Discriminator", s1."Email", s1."Name", s1."PasswordHash", s1."Bio", s1."Specialization"
FROM "SportClasses" AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0."ClassesId", s0."TrainersId", u0."Id", u0."Discriminator", u0."Email", u0."Name", u0."PasswordHash", u0."Bio", u0."Specialization"
    FROM "SportClassTrainer" AS s0
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT u."Id", u."Discriminator", u."Email", u."Name", u."PasswordHash", u."Bio", u."Specialization"
        FROM "Users" AS u
        WHERE u."Discriminator" = 'Trainer'
    ) AS u0 ON s0."TrainersId" = u0."Id"
) AS s1 ON s."Id" = s1."ClassesId"
ORDER BY s."Id", s1."ClassesId", s1."TrainersId"

SELECT s."Id", s."Duration", s."Equipment", s."ImageUrl", s."Level", s."Name", s."TrainerId", s."TrainingType", s1."ClassesId", s1."TrainersId", s1."Id", s1."Discriminator", s1."Email", s1."Name", s1."PasswordHash", s1."Bio", s1."Specialization"
FROM "SportClasses" AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0."ClassesId", s0."TrainersId", u0."Id", u0."Discriminator", u0."Email", u0."Name", u0."PasswordHash", u0."Bio", u0."Specialization"
    FROM "SportClassTrainer" AS s0
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT u."Id", u."Discriminator", u."Email", u."Name", u."PasswordHash", u."Bio", u."Specialization"
        FROM "Users" AS u
        WHERE u."Discriminator" = 'Trainer'
    ) AS u0 ON s0."TrainersId" = u0."Id"
) AS s1 ON s."Id" = s1."ClassesId"
ORDER BY s."Id", s1."ClassesId", s1."TrainersId"

FROM "SportClasses" AS s
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT s0."ClassesId", s0."TrainersId", u0."Id", u0."Discriminator", u0."Email", u0."Name", u0."PasswordHash", u0."Bio", u0."Specialization"
    FROM "SportClassTrainer" AS s0
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT u."Id", u."Discriminator", u."Email", u."Name", u."PasswordHash", u."Bio", u."Specialization"
        FROM "Users" AS u
        WHERE u."Discriminator" = 'Trainer'
    ) AS u0 ON s0."TrainersId" = u0."Id"
) AS s1 ON s."Id" = s1."ClassesId"
ORDER BY s."Id", s1."ClassesId", s1."TrainersId"
